text-pretty"><!--[-->LLMs.txt is a structured documentation format specifically designed for large language models (LLMs). Nuxt provides LLMs.txt files that contain comprehensive information about the framework, making it easy for AI tools to understand and assist with Nuxt development.<!--]--></p><p class="my-5 leading-7 text-pretty"><!--[-->These files are optimized for AI consumption and contain structured information about concepts, APIs, usage patterns, and best practices.<!--]--></p><h2 id="available-routes" class="relative text-2xl text-highlighted font-bold mt-12 mb-6 scroll-mt-[calc(48px+45px+var(--ui-header-height))] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(48px+var(--ui-header-height))] [&amp;&gt;a]:rounded-sm [&amp;&gt;a]:outline-primary/25 [&amp;&gt;a]:focus-visible:outline-3 [&amp;&gt;a&gt;code]:border-dashed hover:[&amp;&gt;a&gt;code]:border-primary hover:[&amp;&gt;a&gt;code]:text-primary [&amp;&gt;a&gt;code]:text-xl/7 [&amp;&gt;a&gt;code]:font-bold [&amp;&gt;a&gt;code]:transition-colors"><a href="#available-routes" class="group lg:after:absolute lg:after:inset-y-0 lg:after:-inset-s-2 lg:after:w-2"><span class="absolute -ms-8 top-1 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 group-focus:opacity-100 p-1 bg-elevated group-hover:text-primary group-focus:text-primary rounded-md hidden lg:flex text-muted transition"><span class="iconify i-lucide:hash size-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" style=""></span></span><!--[-->Available Routes<!--]--></a></h2><p class="my-5 leading-7 text-pretty"><!--[-->We provide LLMs.txt routes to help AI tools access our documentation:<!--]--></p><ul class="list-disc ps-6 my-5 marker:text-(--ui-border-accented)"><!--[--><li class="my-1.5 ps-1.5 leading-7 [&amp;&gt;ul]:my-0"><!--[--><strong><!--[--><code class="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-mono font-medium rounded-md inline-block border border-muted text-highlighted bg-muted"><!--[-->/llms.txt<!--]--></code><!--]--></strong> - Contains a structured overview of all documentation pages and their links (~5K tokens)<!--]--></li><li class="my-1.5 ps-1.5 leading-7 [&amp;&gt;ul]:my-0"><!--[--><strong><!--[--><code class="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-sm font-mono font-medium rounded-md inline-block border border-muted text-highlighted bg-muted"><!--[-->/llms-full.txt<!--]--></code><!--]--></strong> - Provides comprehensive documentation including getting started guides, API references, blog posts, and deployment guides (~1M+ tokens)<!--]--></li><!--]--></ul><h2 id="choosing-the-right-file" class="relative text-2xl text-highlighted font-bold mt-12 mb-6 scroll-mt-[calc(48px+45px+var(--ui-header-height))] lg:scroll-mt-[calc(48px+var(--ui-header-height))] [&amp;&gt;a]:rounded-sm [&amp;&gt;a]:outline-primary/25 [&amp;&gt;a]:focus-visible:outline-3 [&amp;&gt;a&gt;code]:border-dashed hover:[&amp;&gt;a&gt;code]:border-primary hover:[&amp;&gt;a&gt;code]:text-primary [&amp;&gt;a&gt;code]:text-xl/7 [&amp;&gt;a&gt;code]:font-bold [&amp;&gt;a&gt;code]:transition-colors"><a href="#choosing-the-right-file" class="group lg:after:absolute lg:after:inset-y-0 lg:after:-inset-s-2 lg:after:w-2"><span class="absolute -ms-8 top-1 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 group-focus:opacity-100 p-1 bg-elevated group-hover:text-primary group-focus:text-primary rounded-md hidden lg:flex text-muted transition"><span class="iconify i-lucide:hash size-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" style=""></span></span><!--[-->Choosing the Right File<!--]--></a></h2><div class="group relative block px-4 py-3 rounded-md text-sm/6 my-5 last:mb-0 [&amp;_code]:text-xs/5 [&amp;_code]:bg-default [&amp;_pre]:bg-default [&amp;&gt;div]:my-2.5 [&amp;_ul]:my-2.5 [&amp;_ol]:my-2.5 *:last:mb-0!
